TL;DR: 本文利用MUSE光谱数据对边缘朝向的透镜状星系NGC 3957进行动力学与恒星种群分析，识别出主盘、核星盘（NSD）和热成分三个结构；发现NSD最年轻且富金属，表明恒星棒形成于约7 Gyr前；主盘冷动力学特征排除近8 Gyr内显著并合，其年龄梯度支持‘褪色旋涡’演化图景；该星系在小-中等星系群环境中通过内部驱动演化与轻微气体匮乏共同导致‘由外向内’褪色，易被误判为致密星系团中的环境剥离效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解透镜状星系（S0）的形成机制，特别是区分内部演化（如棒驱动）与外部环境效应（如星系团剥离）对星系结构和恒星种群的影响。

Method: 基于GECKOS巡天的深场MUSE积分视场光谱数据，采用尘埃校正的多高斯展开（MGE）与恒星种群-轨道叠加模型，开展动力学分解与恒星年龄、金属丰度反演。

Result: 识别出三个动力学成分：冷主盘（σ_z ~20–30 km/s）、年轻富金属核星盘（t=6.9±0.4 Gyr, [Z/H]=0.49±0.06 dex）和热成分；主盘具强正年龄梯度（内年轻、外年老），且无近期并合迹象；NGC 3957演化路径符合‘褪色旋涡’模型，在小-中等群环境中由内部棒驱动中心恒星形成+外围轻微气体匮乏共同导致‘由外向内’褪色。

Conclusion: NGC 3957并非由致密环境剥离演化而来，而是长期内部 secular 演化与温和环境作用共同塑造的典型S0；其‘由外向内’褪色特征可模拟环境剥离信号，提示需谨慎解读类似观测特征。

Abstract: We analyse the assembly history of the edge-on lenticular galaxy NGC 3957 using deep integral-field spectroscopic MUSE data from the GECKOS survey. By applying a dust-corrected Multi-Gaussian Expansion and a population-orbit superposition model, we disentangle the galaxy's stellar kinematics, age, and metallicity. We dynamically decompose the galaxy and identify three distinct components: a dynamically-cold main disc, a compact Nuclear Stellar Disc (NSD), and a hot component. The NSD emerges as the youngest and most metal-rich component ($t = 6.9 \pm 0.4$ Gyr; $[Z/H] = 0.49 \pm 0.06$ dex), implying that the stellar bar is a long-lived structure that formed at least $\sim 7$ Gyr ago. The main stellar disc is dynamically cold ($σ_z \sim 20-30$ km/s), precluding any significant mergers over the last $\sim 8$ Gyr, and exhibits a strong positive age gradient (younger inside, older outside) beyond the bar radius. Synthesising these dynamical fossil records, NGC 3957 likely evolved as a `faded spiral' in a small-to-medium group environment. Its outer disc might passively fade due to mild gas starvation, while the bar fuelled prolonged central star formation. Comparison with S0s in the Fornax cluster reveals that this combination of internal secular evolution and mild starvation produces `outside-in' fading signatures that could mimic the environmental stripping typically seen in dense clusters.

</details>


### [104] [Super-Eddington Little Blue Dots May Reionize Helium Too Early](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28745)
*Christopher Cain,Brent Smith,Gibson B. Bowling,Yongda Zhu,Lily Whitler,Rafael Ortiz,Anson D'Aloisio,Rogier Windhorst*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）发现的高红移‘小蓝点’（LBDs）是否普遍处于超爱丁顿吸积状态，并通过其对氦再电离时间的影响施加观测约束：若LBDs主导氦再电离，则其超爱丁顿比例应≲10%，否则会与z≈3才结束氦再电离的观测相冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释JWST发现的高红移小蓝点（LBDs）与小红点（LRDs）的物理本质，检验超爱丁顿吸积模型是否自洽，尤其关注其对宇宙氦再电离历史的影响。

Method: 基于超爱丁顿吸积产生强EUV、弱X射线辐射的模型，结合LBDs的光度函数、紫外光度阈值（M_UV < -18）、逃逸分数（15%）及对氦再电离贡献的估算，推导其超爱丁顿占比上限；并分析该约束对EUV谱形和逃逸分数的敏感性。

Result: 若LBDs主导氦再电离且占M_UV < -18星系的5%，则其超爱丁顿比例必须≲10%；若要让多数LBDs为超爱丁顿，需逃逸分数≲1.5%或丰度显著低于当前观测值；结论依赖于超爱丁顿黑洞EUV谱的具体形状。

Conclusion: 当前氦再电离结束时间（z≈3）对LBDs普遍超爱丁顿吸积构成强限制，支持仅一小部分LBDs处于该状态；亟需进一步观测和理论研究以精确刻画其EUV辐射特性。

Abstract: The James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) has identified an abundant population of faint Active Galactic Nuclei (AGN) at $z \gtrsim 4$ which display broad emission lines, compact morphologies, and blue ultraviolet--optical continua. These ``Little Blue Dots' (LBDs) have been suggested to belong to the same family of objects as the more controversial ``Little Red Dots' (LRDs), with differences between the two largely owing to viewing angle effects. In this scenario, super-Eddington accretion resulting in high Extreme UV (EUV) and weak X-ray emission is invoked to explain the properties of both populations. We study the consequences of this super-Eddington scenario for the timing of Helium reionization. We find that observations which support an end to helium reionization no earlier than $z \approx 3$ disfavor scenarios in which the majority of observed $3 \lesssim z \lesssim 7$ LBDs are highly super-Eddington. For our fiducial super-Eddington accretion scenario, we find that the fraction of the LBD population in this state must be $\lesssim 10\%$, assuming LBDs make up $5\%$ of the $M_{ m UV} < -18$ galaxy population and have average escape fractions of $15\%$, comparable to recent observations. Our constraint assumes that LBDs dominate the Helium reionization budget, and would be tighter if bright quasars also contributed significantly. For a majority of LBDs to be super-Eddington, they would need to have small escape fractions ($\lesssim 1.5\%$) and/or be less abundant than observations suggest. Our conclusions are sensitive to the shape of the EUV spectra of super-Eddington black holes, motivating further study.

</details>


### [105] [Shock-heated Away: The Impact of Radiative Cooling on Gas-Phase Transitions in Supernova Remnants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28747)
*Zuzanna Kocjan,Benedikt Diemer,Vadim A. Semenov,Shmuel Bialy,Uri Malamud*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过一系列受控模拟研究了超新星（SN）爆发在不同星际介质（ISM）环境中产生的高温气体量，发现辐射冷却效率是调控高温气体生成的关键因素，并给出了基于环境平均密度、初始相态分布和冷却效率的高温气体峰值质量预测关系，以及冷/暖气体向热相蒸发的物理表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决以往研究中在相似环境条件下对超新星产生高温气体量存在分歧的问题。

Method: 采用涵盖均匀与湍流、多相背景的一系列受控数值模拟，并利用示踪粒子区分冷/暖气体向热相的蒸发过程。

Result: 发现辐射冷却是调控高温气体生成的关键；推导出高温气体峰值质量的预测关系，参数包括平均环境密度、初始相分布及关键温区（10^4.5–10^5.1 K）的冷却时间；给出蒸发效率的物理解析表达式。

Conclusion: 所得简单、可预测的关系可用于星系形成模拟中SN反馈的亚格子模型。

Abstract: Supernova (SN) feedback plays a central role in regulating the structure of the interstellar medium (ISM) through the injection of energy and momentum. The amount of hot gas produced by a supernova is a key quantity that determines how efficiently SN feedback heats the ISM and drives mass exchange between its different gas phases, here defined as cold ($T < 10^3\, \mathrm{K}$), warm ($10^3\, \mathrm{K} < T < 2\times10^4\, \mathrm{K}$), and hot ($T > 2\times10^4\, \mathrm{K}$) gas. However, previous studies have reported discrepant amounts of hot gas formed under otherwise similar ambient conditions. To resolve these disagreements, we quantify the amount of hot gas produced by individual SN explosions using a suite of controlled simulations spanning a broad range of ISM environments that include both uniform and turbulent, multiphase backgrounds. We show that radiative cooling is a key factor regulating hot-gas production, and that differences in cooling efficiency can account for some of the discrepancies reported in the literature. We derive a simple predictive relation for the peak hot-gas mass attained during the evolution of a supernova remnant in terms of the mean ambient density, the initial phase distribution, and the efficiency of gas cooling, which we parameterize as the cooling time over a key temperature range of $10^{4.5}\,\mathrm{K} \lesssim T \lesssim 10^{5.1}\,\mathrm{K}$. Finally, using tracer particles, we distinguish the evaporation of cold and warm gas into the hot phase and derive physically motivated expressions for the evaporation efficiency. Our results provide simple, predictive relations for hot-gas production and phase transitions that can be incorporated into subgrid models of SN feedback in galaxy formation simulations.

</details>


### [106] [Radio-dust connection in quasars driven by powerful ionised outflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28775)
*V. A. Fawcett,C. M. Harrison,T. Costa2,D. M. Alexander,C. Andonie,H. Haidar,R. D. Shepherd,C. L. Sargent,M. J. Temple,C. Circosta,D. J. Rosario*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用DESI光学光谱和LOFAR射电数据，研究类星体中电离外流、尘埃消光与射电辐射之间的关联；发现射电-尘埃关联主要由高速电离外流驱动，而外流速度与光学消光E(B-V)无显著相关，却与g-W2颜色及中红外超（L6μm/L5100Å）呈正相关，表明外流可能通过激波加热尘埃，同时增强中红外和射电辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验电离外流是否驱动观测到的射电-尘埃关联，并探究尘埃或颜色是否影响类星体中高速电离外流的产生。

Method: 基于3418个红移0.5<z<0.9的DESI类星体样本，使用PyQSOFit拟合[OIII]λ5007谱线以约束电离外流速度，并分析其与尘埃消光E(B-V)、光学-中红外颜色g-W2及中红外超L6μm/L5100Å的关系；结合LoTSS DR2射电数据，探索射电、尘埃与外流三者关联。

Result: 射电-尘埃关联主要由含高速电离外流的源主导；[OIII]外流速度与E(B-V)无显著相关，但与g-W2及L6μm/L5100Å呈显著正相关（扣除光度影响后仍成立）。

Conclusion: 高速电离外流通过激波作用加热周围尘埃和气体，从而同时增强中红外和射电辐射，而非尘埃消光本身直接导致外流加速；中红外超是外流活动的重要示踪。

Abstract: Feedback from dusty quasars is thought to be a key driver in galaxy evolution. Multiple studies have reported a link between dust extinction and radio emission in quasars, possibly attributed to winds or jets interacting with the interstellar medium. We use optical spectra from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) to test whether ionised outflows could be driving the observed radio-dust connection and to determine whether dust and/or colour play a role in producing high-velocity ionised outflows in quasars. Based on a sample of 3418 DESI quasars at 0.5 < z < 0.9, we constrained the [OIII]$λ$5007 kinematics by fitting the spectra with PyQSOFit and comparing the observed trends among the [OIII] outflow velocity and the line-of-sight dust extinction E(B-V), optical-to-mid-infrared (MIR) colour g-W2, and L6$μ$m/L5100A (a proxy for MIR excess). Utilising radio data from the LOFAR Two-metre Sky Survey (LoTSS) DR2, we also explored the link between radio, dust, and outflows. We find that the link between radio emission and dust obscuration is driven by sources hosting high-velocity ionised outflows. However, we find no significant trend between the [OIII] outflow velocity and E(B-V), which suggests dust extinction alone is not a significant factor in determining whether a quasar hosts a powerful ionised outflow. On the other hand, we find a clear positive trend between g-W2 and outflow velocity, even after controlling for luminosity. We suggest this is due to a connection between a MIR excess (boosting the W2 band) and outflows, which is supported by a positive trend between L6$μ$m/L5100A and outflow velocity. Our results point to a scenario in which outflows shock the surrounding dust and gas, heating the dust and boosting both the MIR and radio emission.

</details>


### [107] [Estimation of Dust Mass from Infrared Emission and Extinction of Supernova Remnants: G93.7-0.2, G109.1-1.0, G156.2+5.7, and G166.0+4.3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28987)
*Zhe Zhang,Jun Li,Biwei Jiang,He Zhao*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过三维星际消光图和红外光谱能量分布拟合两种互补方法，估算了四个银河系超新星遗迹（SNRs）的尘埃质量，并分析了消光质量与红外发射质量之间的系统性差异及其成因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超新星遗迹是星际尘埃产生和摧毁的主要场所，量化其尘埃质量对理解宇宙尘埃生命周期至关重要。

Method: 采用三维星际消光图法（基于距离区间内消光差异）和红外谱能分布（SED）拟合法（使用WISE、IRAS、AKARI和Planck测光数据，采用‘温+冷’双组分修正黑体模型）估算尘埃质量。

Result: 得到四个SNRs的消光质量分别为108.3、82.0、48.8和119.2 M⊙；SED拟合给出温尘温度43–74 K、冷尘温度13–16 K，冷尘主导红外辐射质量（约90–400 M⊙）；两类质量存在系统性差异。

Conclusion: 消光质量与红外发射质量的差异主要源于视线方向无关前景/背景物质污染，以及尘埃温度和不透明度的不确定性。

Abstract: Supernova remnants (SNRs) are major sites for both the production and destruction of interstellar dust, and quantifying their dust budget is essential for understanding the life cycle of cosmic dust. In this work, the dust masses of four Galactic SNRs (G93.7$-$0.2, G109.1$-$1.0, G156.2+5.7, and G166.0+4.3) are estimated using two complementary methods: the three-dimensional (3D) interstellar extinction map and infrared (IR) spectral energy distribution (SED) fitting based on photometry from WISE, IRAS, AKARI, and Planck. The extinction masses, derived from the differential extinction within each SNR's distance interval, are 108.3, 82.0, 48.8, and 119.2 $M_\odot$, respectively. A two-component (``warm + cold") modified blackbody fitting yields warm dust temperatures of 43--74\,K and cold dust temperatures of 13--16\,K, with the cold dust component dominating the total IR-emission mass ($\sim$90--400 $M_\odot$). The extinction masses and IR emission masses show systematic differences, likely caused by sightline contamination from unrelated foreground/background material and uncertainties in dust temperatures and opacities.

</details>


### [108] [Can Little Red Dots Contribute To The Early Universe Cosmic Dust Budget?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29011)
*Chris Ashall,Rohan P. Naidu,Alberto Torralba,Irene Shivaei,John Chisholm,Hanpu Liu,Zhaoran Liu,Jorryt Matthee,Kyle Medler,Tyco Mera,Takashi J. Moriya,Devesh Nandal,Robert A. Simcoe,Wendy Q. Sun*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了早期宇宙中尘埃起源的不确定性，提出小红点（LRDs）可能作为高红移尘埃产生的重要渠道，并通过类比IIn型超新星及建模低红移LRD类似体的SED，支持其内部风或冷密壳层中可形成大量尘埃（~10²–10³ M⊙），且该尘埃可能避免被激波摧毁、利于生长；种群估算表明LRDs对早期宇宙尘埃生产的贡献可从可忽略到主导不等，甚至在某些条件下超过核心坍缩超新星（CCSNe）的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期宇宙中尘埃起源尚不明确，而小红点（LRDs）因其特殊性质可能提供新的高红移尘埃产生机制，本文受IIn型超新星高效产尘现象启发，探究LRDs是否具备类似条件。

Method: 基于IIn型超新星产尘机制类比，构建LRDs外流与冷密壳层中的尘埃形成模型；对两个低红移LRD类似体进行SED拟合（热伪光球+光学薄尘埃成分），并结合窄线发射（Case B比值、低金属丰度）排除宿主ISM主导尘埃来源；开展光学深度、升华半径和能量平衡检验；最后进行种群统计估算其宇宙学尺度产尘贡献。

Result: 两个低红移LRD类似体拟合得到尘埃质量达10²–10³ M⊙；观测特征（窄线比例、金属丰度）支持尘埃形成于LRD自身外流而非宿主ISM；模型满足物理自洽性约束；种群计算显示LRDs对z>5尘埃生产的贡献可在极小至主导间变化，高效率下可接近或超过CCSNe下限贡献。

Conclusion: LRDs可作为早期宇宙的重要尘埃工厂，在特定条件下甚至可能主导尘埃生产或提供种子颗粒供后续ISM中生长，为解决早期宇宙尘埃丰度难题提供了新路径。

Abstract: The origin of dust in the early Universe remains uncertain. We explore whether Little Red Dots (LRDs) could provide a high-redshift dust-production channel. Motivated by an analogy with Type IIn supernovae, we investigate whether LRDs may share the efficient dust-forming conditions. We consider dust formation in outer winds and later in a shielded cold dense shell after the central source fades. Such dust may avoid a global remnant-phase reverse shock, while longer LRD lifetimes may promote grain growth. We model the SEDs of two low-redshift LRD analogs as a thermal pseudo-photosphere plus optically thin dust components, obtaining dust reservoirs of order $10^{2}$-$10^{3}\,M_{\odot}$. Although photometry cannot exclude pre-existing dust, the narrow Balmer components of both analogs are close to the Case B ratio and their narrow-line environments are metal-poor, disfavoring a dominant diffuse host-ISM origin for the inferred reservoir. Formation within the LRD outflow is consistent with our optical-depth, sublimation, and energy-balance checks. In this scenario, the large dust masses do not produce a strong optical attenuation because a clumpy or asymmetric distribution can leave the dominant optical sightlines relatively unobscured. Our population calculation shows that the LRD contribution to early-Universe dust production can range from negligible to dominant. At higher efficiencies, LRDs can approach or exceed the lower CCSN contribution above $z\approx5$, whereas less favorable assumptions yield a minor contribution. We therefore propose that LRDs can act as early-Universe dust factories and, under some conditions, may dominate over CCSNe or provide seed grains for subsequent growth in the early ISM.

</details>


### [109] [The Cygnus Allscale Survey of Chemistry and Dynamical Environments: CASCADE: VI. Molecular outflows in the DR21 ridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29190)
*I. M. Skretas,A. Karska,F. Wyrowski,H. Beuther,W. -J. Kim,C. Gieser,D. A. Semenov,A. Hernández-Gómez,N. Schneider,T. Csengeri,A. Hacar*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了DR21脊上原恒星外流特性，发现即使在极端密集的恒星形成区，外流性质仍与已知规律一致，仅在大尺度吸积丝状结构交汇处的源驱动最强外流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究恒星形成环境（如DR21脊这样的高密度、大质量星形成区）是否影响恒星形成过程，特别是通过分子外流这一可观测示踪体来评估环境效应。

Method: 利用IRAM 30m和NOEMA望远镜获取的HCO+ J=1-0、H13CO+ J=1-0和SiO J=2-1观测数据，在DR21脊上识别分子外流；计算外流物理性质，并与文献中低至高质量产外流样本进行统计对比。

Result: 在DR21脊34个致密核中识别出14个（41%）具有分子外流；外流性质整体符合已有外流-源关联规律；除位于大尺度（~1 pc）吸积丝状结构交汇处的源驱动最强外流外，其余源外流性质变化很小。

Conclusion: 原恒星外流性质在极端致密、团簇环境（如DR21脊）下基本不受环境影响，表明恒星形成过程（至少在外流阶段）具有普适性，环境主要通过调控大尺度物质输运（如吸积丝）间接影响个别源。

Abstract: Star formation takes place in varied environments, from isolated clumps to massive molecular cloud complexes. However, whether the environment in which a star forms has any effect on the formation process remains a matter of debate. The molecular outflows, launched during the formation of protostars present a more easily accessible way to study star formation in different environments. The DR21 ridge, in the Cygnus-X high-mass star-forming complex, hosts a high number of massive dense cores and embedded protostars with outflows. We aim to identify molecular outflows associated with dense molecular cores along the DR21 ridge, and investigate whether the extended environment impacts the formation process of stars within it. We identified molecular outflows along the DR21 ridge using HCO+ J=1-0, H13CO+ J=1-0, and SiO J=2-1 observations obtained with the IRAM 30m telescope and NOEMA as part of the CASCADE program. We calculated outflow properties and performed statistical comparisons between the DR21 ridge sources and a literature sample of low- to high-mass outflow sources. Based on the morphology of HCO+, H13CO+ and SiO, we identify molecular outflows in 14 out of 34 dense cores (41%) along the DR21 ridge. Despite the high density of star formation in DR21, the resulting outflow properties are found to be in good agreement with the established correlations between outflow and source properties. Little variation is seen in the outflow properties of sources along the ridge, with the exception of sources located at the intersection of the DR21 ridge with large-scale (~1 pc) accretion filaments. These sources are found to drive the most powerful outflows. Overall, our results indicate that protostellar outflow properties, even when driven by sources forming in an extreme and clustered region, such as the DR21 ridge, remain largely unaffected.

</details>


### [110] [Tracing Radial Migration in the Outer Disk: A Comprehensive Analysis of the Old Open Cluster Berkeley 36](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29637)
*Deniz Cennet Çınar,D. Bisht,Ing-Guey Jiang,K. Belwal,Selçuk Bilir*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文对古老疏散星团Berkeley 36进行了化学运动学、结构与测光分析，利用Gaia DR3和Gaia-ESO Survey数据，确认了946颗高置信度成员星，测得其年龄约6.8–7.75 Gyr、金属丰度[Fe/H]=−0.19 dex、轨道位于银河系外盘并经历了约5 kpc的向外径向迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决古老疏散星团中年龄-消光-金属丰度简并性问题，并探究其在银河系演化中的动力学历史与径向迁移特征。

Method: 结合Gaia DR3天体测量与测光数据及Gaia-ESO Survey高分辨率光谱，采用高斯混合模型识别成员星，拟合等时线确定年龄，利用Ba基化学钟交叉验证，并通过轨道积分与银河系势模型分析径向迁移。

Result: 获得946颗高概率成员星；核心半径2.63±0.21角分，潮汐半径14.84±0.47角分；年龄6.8±0.5 Gyr（等时线）与7.75±1.94 Gyr（Ba钟）；金属丰度[Fe/H]=−0.19±0.02 dex；主序双星比例48.0±1.7%；蓝离散星83颗且空间分布延展；轨道半径R_GC=11.42±0.44 kpc，出生半径R_b=6.71±0.66 kpc，表明约5 kpc向外迁移。

Conclusion: Berkeley 36是一个动力学弛豫、中等集中的古老星团，其显著的径向迁移特征使其成为研究银河系盘演化、径向迁移机制与古老疏散星团长期演化的基准样本。

Abstract: We present a chemo-kinematical, structural, and photometric analysis of the old open cluster Berkeley 36 using Gaia DR3 astrometry and photometry together with high-resolution spectroscopy from the Gaia-ESO Survey DR5.1. Applying a Gaussian Mixture Model to Gaia astrometry, we identify 946 high-probability cluster members. We derive a core radius of 2.63 +/- 0.21 arcmin and a tidal radius of 14.84 +/- 0.47 arcmin, indicating a moderately concentrated and dynamically relaxed system. By fixing the cluster metallicity to the spectroscopic value of [Fe/H] = -0.19 +/- 0.02 dex and adopting an independently determined geometric distance of 4377 +/- 510 pc, we minimize the classical age-reddening-metallicity degeneracy and derive a robust isochrone age of 6.8 +/- 0.5 Gyr. Independent Ba-based chemical clocks yield a mean age of 7.75 +/- 1.94 Gyr, supporting the isochrone solution. The cluster exhibits a high main-sequence binary fraction of 48.0 +/- 1.7% and hosts 83 blue straggler star candidates with an extended spatial distribution, contrary to classical mass segregation. Orbit integration shows that Berkeley 36 follows a nearly circular orbit in the outer Galactic disc at R_GC = 11.42 +/- 0.44 kpc. Accounting for the Galactic warp and disc flare increases the cluster's maximum vertical excursion by 86% and the local disc scale height by 17%, respectively. Comparison with its chemically inferred birth radius at R_b = 6.71 +/- 0.66 kpc indicates an outward radial migration of approximately 5 kpc, predominantly driven by churning. These results establish Berkeley 36 as a benchmark for investigating radial migration, secular evolution, and the dynamical history of old Galactic open clusters.

</details>


### [111] [The multi-frequency radio light curve of the sub-pc SMBH binary candidate PG 1302-102](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29682)
*Sayan Basu,Roger P. Deane,Keith W. Bannister*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文分析了类星体PG 1302-102长达19年的多频射电光变曲线，发现其5.5 GHz射电流量无显著周期性，但其带内谱指数存在与光学一致的~5.2年周期性及近期变化，暗示射电与光学周期性物理耦合，支持其为亚秒差距超大质量双黑洞（SMBHB）系统的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证PG 1302-102中观测到的光学周期性是否源于超大质量双黑洞（SMBHB），并探索射电与光学波段周期性之间是否存在物理关联，以增强SMBHB候选体的证认可靠性。

Method: 基于ATCA望远镜2005–2024年约19年的多频射电观测数据，构建PG 1302-102的射电光变曲线和5.5 GHz带内谱指数时间序列，采用时域分析方法（如Lomb-Scargle周期图）检验周期性，并与已有光学周期信号进行比对。

Result: 5.5 GHz射电流量无显著周期性；但其带内谱指数时间序列展现出与光学一致的~5.2年周期性，且近期报道的光学周期变化也在射电谱指数中得到复现。

Conclusion: 射电谱指数与光学光变的周期性耦合虽机制不同，却高度一致，强化了PG 1302-102作为亚秒差距SMBHB系统的解释，并为LSST与SKAO联合探测时代提供了重要先例。

Abstract: Supermassive black hole binary (SMBHB) candidates with sub-parsec (sub-pc) orbital separations are rare, high-value systems, thought to be the dominant sources of the recently detected nanoHertz (nHz) gravitational wave (GW) background. Alongside pulsar-timing-array searches for localized nHz GW sources from SMBHBs, an increasing number of candidates have been identified from synoptic surveys of large quasar optical light curves over increasingly long baselines. The optical light curve of the quasar PG~1302-102 at a redshift of $z=0.278$ exhibits a pronounced, smoothly varying periodic signal of ~5.2 years over approximately 20 years. Although the cause of this periodicity remains debated, a plausible explanation is a binary system of two SMBHs with a sub-pc separation. At this close distance, the $10^{8.3-9.4} M_{\odot}$ nuclear black holes in PG 1302-102 are expected to merge within $\sim$$10^{5}$ yr solely due to GW emission. We present a multi-frequency radio light curve analysis of PG~1302-102 spanning a $\sim$19-year baseline, with observations between 2005 and 2024 drawn from the Australian Telescope Compact Array (ATCA). The ATCA radio light curve is highly variable at 5.5~GHz but shows no significant evidence of the periodicity seen at optical wavelengths. However, we report fractionally stronger evidence of periodicity in the in-band spectral index time-series at 5.5~GHz, with a period consistent with the optical periodicity. Furthermore, the recently reported change in optical periodicity is also present in the radio in-band spectral index light curve. These two results suggest that radio and optical periodicity may be physically coupled despite very different emission mechanisms. We argue that this radio-optical coupling reinforces the SMBHB interpretation, despite the optical periodicity change, and provides an intriguing case study ahead of the LSST-SKAO era.

</details>


### [112] [Primordial Black Hole Seeds for Little Red Dots in $f(R)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29727)
*Saeed Fakhry*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在Hu-Sawicki f(R) 引力理论框架下，通过原初黑洞（PBH）在致密星团中分层并合形成中等质量种子黑洞，再经气体吸积成长的混合黑洞组装模型，成功解释JWST观测到的高红移‘小红点’（LRDs）和超大质量黑洞的早期丰度问题，并利用多信使观测打破参数简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST在高红移处发现大量紧凑型小红点（LRDs）和超大质量黑洞，对标准爱丁顿极限下由恒星残骸缓慢增长的黑洞形成模型构成严重时间挑战。

Method: 构建一种统一的混合组装框架：在f(R)修正引力下，原初黑洞在致密星团中通过分层并合形成中等质量种子黑洞；利用其第五力增强引力波辐射捕获率，并通过环境依赖的变色龙屏蔽机制自调节生长；随后叠加星系气体吸积演化。结合JWST观测的LRD空间密度、LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA限制及三种多信使诊断（并合残余自旋、随机引力波背景截断、PBH空间关联函数尺度依赖反转）进行模型检验与参数约束。

Result: 该模型仅需极低的PBH丰度（f_PBH ≲ 10⁻³）和合理活动星系核占空比（δ ≈ 10⁻²），即可自然复现z ∼ 5.5–6.5处JWST观测到的LRD空间密度；同时满足当前引力波探测器对PBH的限制，且f(R)修正引力自然驱动质量分布的高质端。三种多信使信号可清晰打破f(R)参数与星团密度之间的简并。

Conclusion: f(R)修正引力下的PBH分层并合+气体吸积路径为解决高红移超大质量黑洞的‘时间危机’提供了物理自洽、观测可证的替代方案；多信使天文学是甄别此类新物理的关键途径。

Abstract: The discovery by the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) of an abundant population of compact little red dots (LRDs) and supermassive black holes at high redshifts poses a severe timing challenge for standard Eddington-limited growth from stellar remnants. In this work, we present a unified hybrid assembly framework in which intermediate-mass seed black holes are formed via hierarchical primordial black hole (PBH) mergers within dense clusters in Hu-Sawicki $f(R)$ gravity, followed by galactic gas accretion. We demonstrate that the screened $f(R)$ fifth force nonlinearly accelerates early seed formation through a quadratic enhancement of the gravitational-wave radiation-capture kernel, while environmental chameleon screening dynamically self-regulates the growth. Because subsequent gas accretion scales multiplicatively with seed mass, the $f(R)$ seed enhancement is preserved throughout the accretion history, drastically reducing the time-averaged Eddington ratios required to assemble $10^{6}\text{-}10^{9}\,M_{\odot}$ LRDs by cosmic dawn. Confronting our active mass functions with JWST data reveals that a small clustered PBH fraction ($f_{\rm PBH} \lesssim 10^{-3}$) under a realistic active galactic nucleus duty cycle ($δ\approx 10^{-2}$) naturally matches observed LRD space densities at $z \sim 5.5\text{-}6.5$ while remaining fully compliant with LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA limits, with modified gravity naturally driving the high-mass tail. Finally, we show that parameter degeneracies between modified gravity and cluster density can be cleanly broken by combining three multi-messenger diagnostics: remnant spin state signatures, a stochastic GW background cutoff, and a scale-dependent inversion in the PBH spatial correlation function.

</details>


### [113] [The Structural Abundance Crisis of Massive Galaxies in Current Cosmological Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29893)
*Hassen M. Yesuf,Connor Bottrell,Luis C. Ho,Aaron S. G. Robotham,Sabine Bellstedt,Robin H. W. Cook,Lei Hao,Fengshan Liu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过对比观测数据与多个宇宙学模拟（IllustrisTNG、EAGLE、SIMBA）生成的星系图像，构建结构丰度函数（SAFs），发现所有模拟在星系形态参数（如Sérsic指数、集中度、尺寸、椭率）上均存在显著系统偏差，尤其普遍低估高度扁平的盘状星系，表明当前星系形成模型虽能复现整体质量函数，却未能准确刻画内部结构演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有星系形成模拟能在全局统计量（如恒星质量函数）上与观测一致，但其对星系内部结构（反映气体吸积、恒星形成、反馈及并合等物理过程）的刻画是否可靠仍不清楚；需发展更严格的检验基准。

Method: 利用Subaru Hyper Suprime-Cam巡天观测数据与IllustrisTNG、EAGLE、SIMBA模拟生成的匹配伪图像，在统一成像处理流程下，构建多个关键形态参数（Sérsic指数、浓度、有效半径、椭率）的结构丰度函数（SAFs），进行定量比较。

Result: 所有模拟在各形态参数的SAFs上均呈现>5σ系统偏差（RMSE达0.2–1.8 dex），丰度差异达数倍；共同失败是严重低估高度扁平盘星系；TNG表现最优，SIMBA偏差最大。

Conclusion: 仅依赖积分观测量（如总光度、质量）的一致性不足以验证星系形成模型的物理真实性；结构丰度函数（SAFs）可作为多维、可观测、高敏感性的新基准，推动下一代模型在角动量与质量再分布物理上的改进。

Abstract: The internal structure of galaxies encodes the complex baryon cycle driven by gas accretion, star formation, and feedback, together with secular evolution and environmentally driven processes such as mergers and tidal interactions. We present a population-level census of massive nearby galaxies ($\log(M_\star/M_\odot) > 10$) by comparing Hyper Suprime-Cam Subaru Strategic Program observations with matched mock images from IllustrisTNG, EAGLE, and SIMBA. Using a consistent, like-for-like imaging pipeline, we construct structural abundance functions (SAFs) for key morphological parameters, revealing a structural abundance crisis. Across Sérsic index, concentration, size, and ellipticity, all simulations exhibit large, systematic discrepancies ($>5σ$; RMSE $\sim 0.2$--$1.8$\,dex), typically corresponding to abundance differences of factors of several. While individual simulations display diverse failures---underproducing or overproducing compact spheroids, extended or round galaxies ---all underproduce highly flattened disks. Although TNG shows the closest agreement and SIMBA the largest offsets, this shared failure indicates that current models---despite matching global demographics such as the stellar mass function---do not uniquely constrain internal galaxy structure. Our results demonstrate that agreement in integrated observables can mask fundamental shortcomings in the modelling of mass and angular momentum redistribution. We therefore establish SAFs as a stringent, multidimensional, and observationally accessible benchmark for testing and calibrating next-generation galaxy formation models in the era of upcoming deep, wide-field surveys.

</details>


### [114] [Multiscale Synthetic observations of Polarized dust emission: On the origin of Depolarization effect from Molecular clouds to Starless cores and Constraints on Dust Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30009)
*Nguyen Chau Giang,Thiem Hoang,Blakesley Burkhart,Doris Arzoumanian,Nguyen Bich Ngoc,Jihye Hwang,Aran Lyo*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过POLARIS模拟研究了分子云到无星核中尘埃偏振分数随发射强度下降（p-I关系）的物理机制，指出磁场几何效应在低柱密度下主导退偏振，而尘埃颗粒生长和磁性（如超顺磁性）共同决定高柱密度下对齐效率损失是否成为主导机制，并提出可通过p-I斜率和核心中心平均偏振度识别退偏振机制与尘埃物理性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释观测中普遍存在的偏振分数p随尘埃发射强度I下降的现象，厘清磁场几何效应与尘埃颗粒对齐损失各自贡献。

Method: 使用POLARIS代码开展850微米多尺度合成偏振观测，基于RATs机制模拟磁取向尘埃，构建三种不同磁能水平的坍缩云模型，系统考察尘埃磁性（顺磁/超顺磁）与粒径增长（>0.5μm）的影响。

Result: 发现：1）NH<10^21–10^22 cm^-2时，磁场几何效应主导退偏振；2）若尘埃粒径>0.5μm且为含大铁团簇的超顺磁体，RATs在高柱密度仍有效，对齐损失仅在NH>10^23 cm^-2起作用；3）否则，对齐损失或气体阻尼导致的对齐效率下降在NH>10^22 cm^-2即成主导；4）p-I斜率与核心中心平均p可诊断退偏振机制及尘埃物理性质。

Conclusion: 退偏振机制高度依赖于尘埃微观物理属性（粒径与磁性），而非仅由宏观磁场结构决定；观测上p-I关系可作为探测星前核内尘埃性质与磁场耦合状态的有效工具。

Abstract: Submillimeter observations of polarized dust emission from molecular clouds to starless cores frequently report a decrease in polarization fraction (p) with increasing dust emission intensity (I). This feature is commonly attributed to the alignment loss of dust grains or the geometrical effects of magnetic fields, yet, the detailed contributions remain unclear. To investigate the mechanism responsible for the depolarization, we use POLARIS to perform the multiscale synthetic dust polarization observations at $850μm$ from magnetically aligned dust grains by RAdiative Torques (RATs) mechanism. We adopt three collapsing cloud models with different magnetic energy levels and explore the effects of grain magnetic properties and grain growth on dust polarization. The field geometrical effect is the dominant depolarization mechanism at $N_{\rm H}<10^{21}-10^{22}\rm cm^{-2}$. We find that if grains grow beyond $>0.5μm$ and are superparamagnetic (SPM) with large iron clusters, RATs remain effective at high column densities, and grain alignment loss contributes to depolarization only at $N_{\rm H} > 10^{23}\rm cm^{-2}$. If neither of these conditions is satisfied, the alignment loss (in cases of insufficient grain growth); or the reduced grain alignment efficiency by gaseous damping (for paramagnetic grains or SPM grains with small iron cluster sizes) can become the dominant depolarization mechanism at $N_{\rm H} > 10^{22}\rm cm^{-2}$, regardless of how tangled the magnetic field lines in our simulation. Finally, we show that the depolarization mechanism and the underlying dust physical properties inside starless cores may be identified through the $p-I$ slope and the mean p at the core center.

</details>


### [115] [Caught Napping by JWST UNCOVER+MegaScience: Constraining bursty star formation histories and number densities of mini-quenched galaxies at redshifts 4-7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30011)
*Gourav Khullar,Rachel Bezanson,Katherine A. Suess,Ikki Mitsuhashi,David J. Setton,Joel Leja,Sedona H. Price,Katherine E. Whitaker,Emilie Burnham,John R. Weaver,Iryna Chemerynska,Lukas J. Furtak,Jenny Greene,Bingjie Wang,Hakim Atek,Gabe Brammer,Olivia R. Cooper,Robert Feldmann,Seiji Fujimoto,Anna de Graaff,Ivo Labbe,Danilo Marchesini,Ian McConachie,Tim B. Miller,Abby Mintz,Themiya Nanayakkara,Pascal Oesch,Richard Pan,Casey Papovich,Yunchong Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用UNCOVER/MegaScience巡天数据，通过光谱和测光方法识别出处于短暂‘小休眠’（napping）状态的星系，并结合Prospector非参数模型推断其恒星形成历史，发现这类星系在SFR₁₀/SFR₁₀₀参数空间中具有独特分布；研究证实中等波段测光可单独高效识别该瞬变相，并在z=4–7测得其数密度，支持低质量星系中随机恒星形成与恒星反馈在再电离后数百兆年内的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解再电离后低质量星系中恒星形成的随机性及其受恒星反馈调控的机制，需观测处于短暂淬灭过渡态（即‘napping’）的星系，但该相寿命短、难捕捉，亟需可靠识别方法。

Method: 基于UNCOVER/MegaScience巡天的光谱与中等波段测光数据，选取具中等巴耳末断裂、弱Hα发射线（EW<100Å）和蓝紫外连续谱的星系；采用Prospector灵活非参数模型拟合星系SFR时序，聚焦爆发式形成与快速淬灭；利用SFR₁₀/SFR₁₀₀参数空间定位nappers，并验证测光识别纯样本的能力。

Result: 在Abell 2744透镜场z=4–7发现8个光谱确认与60个测光候选napping星系（质量范围log₁₀(M*/M⊙)=7.5–10）；证实中等波段测光可独立、纯净识别该相；nappers最常见于log₁₀(M*/M⊙)≈9的低质量端；数密度或随红移从z∼6增至z∼4，但受限于宇宙方差。

Conclusion: napping星系是探测再电离后数百兆年内随机恒星形成与恒星反馈强度及占空比的关键观测探针；中等波段测光已具备高效识别该瞬变相的能力，为未来大视场巡天（如Rubin、Euclid）提供方法学支撑。

Abstract: We explore the prevalence of mini-quenched or ``napping'' galaxies selected from spectroscopic and photometric samples in the UNCOVER/MegaScience survey. These galaxies are empirically identified by the presence of moderate Balmer breaks, weak emission lines ($EW(Hα) < 100$A) and relatively blue UV continua. We infer the star formation histories (SFHs) of our sample using flexible non-parametric models with Prospector optimized to capture recent episodes of bursty star formation and quenching, and find that they are uniquely identifiable in the SFR$_{10}$/SFR$_{100}$ parameter space moving towards (temporary) quiescence. We demonstrate that although spectroscopy is best able to identify rapidly declining SFRs, densely sampled medium-band photometry recover these key spectral features and thus robustly identify pure samples of this transient phase -- with imaging alone. We quantify the number density of napping galaxies at $z=4-7$ in the Abell 2744 lensing field, finding 8 spectroscopically confirmed nappers and 60 photometric candidates spanning log$_{10}$(M$_*$/M$_\odot$) $= 7.5-10$. We verify that the photometry alone can identify a pure sample of nappers, leveraging a smaller high signal-to-noise ratio spectroscopic sample. Consistent with previous studies, we find that nappers are most common at low stellar mass (log$_{10}$(M$_*$/M$_\odot$) $\sim9$). We see a hint that the number densities increase from $z\sim6$ to $z\sim4$, though our small sample is likely affected by cosmic variance. Our study demonstrates the increasing importance of stochastic star formation as a regulator of low-mass galaxy growth in the several hundred Myr after reionization, and offers a direct observational testbed for the strength and duty cycle of stellar feedback in cosmological simulations.

</details>


### [116] [Testing SALT Approximations with Numerical Radiative Transfer Code. II. Thermal and Microturbulent Line Broadening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30312)
*Cody Carr,Renyue Cen,Leo Michel-Dansac,Claudia Scarlata,Alaina Henry*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文扩展了SALT模型，引入热和湍流运动导致的谱线展宽，在单次散射近似下求解辐射转移方程，并用逃逸概率法处理再发射；验证表明该改进显著提升了风参数（如离子柱密度、质量外流率）的恢复精度，但仍存在多普勒展宽参数与径向密度/速度分布之间的简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前向模型（如基于Sobolev近似的SALT）常忽略热与湍流运动引起的谱线展宽，导致从观测中反演星系风基本性质时产生偏差。

Method: 在SALT框架中引入速度弥散效应，于单次散射极限下求解辐射转移方程；采用类似Sobolev近似的逃逸概率法处理共振区有限厚度内的光子再发射。

Result: 发现Doppler展宽参数与径向密度/速度梯度之间存在简并：较平缓的梯度可模拟更大速度弥散的效果；但积分量恢复良好：Si+离子柱密度恢复散差0.26 dex、系统高估0.22 dex；终端半径处质量外流率散差0.88 dex、系统高估0.51 dex。

Conclusion: 新模型显著优于旧版SALT，在保持计算效率的同时提高了关键风物理量的反演精度，但需谨慎处理参数简并问题。

Abstract: Forward models that connect galactic winds to their predicted spectral-line profiles have proved effective for inferring wind properties in controlled settings, but important limitations remain. In particular, many models rely on the Sobolev approximation to solve the radiative transfer equation and neglect line broadening caused by thermal and turbulent motions within the wind. In the first paper of this series, we demonstrated that neglecting this broadening in Semi-Analytical Line Transfer (SALT) models can bias the recovery of fundamental wind properties from mock observations. Here, we extend the SALT framework to incorporate this motion by solving the radiative transfer equation in the single-scattering limit. We treat re-emission using an escape-probability approach similar to that adopted under the Sobolev approximation, while allowing photons to escape from resonance regions of finite thickness. We validate the model and investigate parameter degeneracies by fitting mock spectra generated with Monte Carlo radiative transfer simulations assuming identical outflow configurations. We identify a degeneracy between the Doppler-broadening parameter and the radial density and velocity profiles: shallower density and velocity gradients can mimic the effects of greater velocity dispersion. Nevertheless, integrated quantities are well recovered. Over the range $13 \leq \log(N_{\mathrm{Si}^+}/\mathrm{cm}^{-2}) \leq 18$, the recovered ionic column densities have a scatter of 0.26 dex and are systematically overestimated by 0.22 dex. Mass-outflow rates evaluated at the terminal wind radius have a scatter of 0.88 dex and are systematically overestimated by 0.51 dex. These results represent substantial improvements over previous versions of the model.

</details>


### [117] [Broad-band host measurements of little red dots and similar compact nuclei require source-conditioned polychromatic PSFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30459)
*Sergio Bonaque-González*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文指出，使用场星建模点扩散函数（PSF）来解析高红移致密天体结构时，若目标源与场星光谱不同，会导致系统性光度误分配，从而错误估计其延展成分、尺寸、光度和质量；作者明确了该误差的物理与数学根源，并提出一种普适性修正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高红移致密天体的结构表征易受PSF建模误差影响，尤其当目标源光谱与用于PSF建模的场星不同时，现有方法可能导致系统性偏差，进而影响关键物理量推断。

Method: 以Zhang等人研究的‘小红点’（LRDs）为测试案例，分析在宽带滤光片下使用场星PSF建模对光谱差异目标的影响，并从物理和数学角度推导误差来源，进而提出并验证一种修正方法。

Result: 证实了光谱差异会系统性误分配光通量，导致延展成分被高估或虚假产生；该误差显著改变所推断的尺寸、光度和质量；提出了可推广至其他致密遥远源的修正方案。

Conclusion: 基于场星的PSF建模在处理光谱不同的高红移源时存在固有缺陷，必须进行光谱匹配或校正，否则将导致物理诠释严重失真；所提修正方法可提升此类分析的可靠性。

Abstract: Characterizing compact sources in the early Universe is difficult because light from physically distinct components can overlap within the same diffraction-limited image. A common method uses field stars to model how a single point of light appears and from this estimates the intrinsic structure and contributions of the physical components of the distant object under study. Many important properties inferred for high-redshift objects depend on the accuracy of this method. Using the little red dots (LRDs) analysed by Zhang et al. as a test case, this work shows that applying the method through broad filters to objects whose spectra differ from those of the field stars can systematically misassign light, overestimating an existing extended component or creating an apparent one where none exists. At the measured level, this error is large enough to alter the inferred sizes, luminosities and masses, and therefore the physical interpretation of these systems. The physical and mathematical origin of the error is established and a correction applicable to other compact distant sources is demonstrated.

</details>


### [118] [Photometric redshifts for active galactic nuclei with LePHARE for the Vera C. Rubin Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30494)
*R. Shirley,M. Salvato,J. Cohen-Tanugi,O. Ilbert,S. Arnouts,R. Ansari,R. Assef,M. Banerji,A. Bongiorno,W. N. Brandt,J. Buchner,J. Comparat,D. Ilić,A. Kovačević,J. Kubica,B. Laloux,O. Lynn,A. Malz,L. Marchetti,C. Mazzucchelli,T. Mkrtchyan,K. Nandra,D. Oldag,C. Ricci,W. Roster,E. Saremi,S. Satheesh-Sheeba,T. Tasnim. Ananna,M. J. Temple,M. Vaccari,A. Viitanen,C. Wolf,Z. Yu,T. Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了在大型巡天项目（如LSST）中，如何更准确地测定活动星系核（AGN）的测光红移，指出使用星系模板处理点源AGN会导致结果不可靠，并提出了针对不同类型AGN的优化配置及相应性能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型巡天项目（如LSST）将发现大量AGN，但现有测光红移分析工具未针对AGN特性优化，且有限波段数和错误模板假设严重影响其红移精度。

Method: 利用LePHARE代码，在COSMOS场六类AGN样本（X射线、射电、红外、变源、颜色、光谱选源）上测试HSC-CLAUDS多波段数据；开发并集成LSST版LePHARE至RAIL框架；发布28波段COSMOS AGN测光红移及后验分布。

Result: 发现用星系模板拟合点源AGN会导致高度不可靠的测光红移；浅层全天天文巡天（如eROSITA、WISE、ZTF）遗漏大量AGN，致其被误认为星系；提供了按AGN类型划分的模板配置建议及性能指标（随红移、星等、选源方式变化）。

Conclusion: AGN测光红移需专用模板与方法；本文提供可复现的配置方案、性能基准及公开数据集，为LSST等未来巡天中的AGN科学分析奠定基础。

Abstract: Active Galactic Nuclei (AGN) play a crucial role in galaxy evolution, but they are a minority of extragalactic sources with diverse Spectral Energy Distributions (SEDs), which depend on their means of selection. Upcoming large-scale surveys such as LSST will identify many AGN, but analysis tools are not optimized for them. The limited number of photometric bands in these surveys impacts the calculation of photometric redshifts for AGN, which are essential for scientific advancement.
  We use LePHARE to demonstrate the impact that a limited number of bands and erroneous assumptions have on the determination of the photometric redshifts of AGN. We conduct tests on six AGN samples selected using X-ray, radio, infrared, variability, color, and spectroscopic criteria in the COSMOS field, using photometry from HSC-CLAUDS, which is closest in depth and wavelength coverage to LSST.
  We present the LSST pipeline for LePHARE within the Redshift Assessment Infrastructure Layers (RAIL), facilitating comparison between SED fitting and machine learning algorithms.
  AGN that appear as point-like sources in optical data will be assigned highly unreliable photometric redshifts if they are processed using galaxy templates. Additionally, shallow all-sky surveys (like eROSITA, WISE, and ZTF) miss many AGN. As a result, these ``hidden'' AGN are often misidentified as galaxies in public survey data, leading to incorrect photometric redshift.
  We provide the configurations that are suggested for each type of AGN alongside measures of expected performance as a function of redshift, magnitude, and selection. To facilitate studies with a panchromatic view of AGN, we also release photometric redshifts and posterior distributions for all AGN sources identified in the COSMOS field using the six criteria, based on 28-band photometry.

</details>


### [119] [Constraining Baryonic Feedback at $z\sim 1$ with the H$α$ Luminosity Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30698)
*Ivan Rapoport,Vincent Desjacques,Ehud Behar,Shmuel Bialy*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用Illustris和IllustrisTNG模拟研究了重子反馈对Hα光度函数（LF）的影响，考虑了尘埃衰减和Lyman系光子传播的物理模型，并区分了H II区、弥散ISM碰撞激发/复合及AGN光致电离三类Hα辐射源；通过单参数校准后发现弥散气体贡献约1%-10%总Hα光度，AGN主导极高光度端（L_Hα ≳ 3×10⁴² erg/s），而低反馈的IllustrisTNG能更好再现z∼1时观测到的LF，高反馈的Illustris则存在亮暗端拟合张力；该结果凸显Hα LF与kSZ测量联合约束反馈物理的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重子反馈是星系形成模型中的主要不确定性之一，尤其影响星际介质（ISM）和恒星形成，但其对观测关键探针——如Hα光度函数（LF）——的具体影响尚不清晰，亟需通过先进模拟与多成分辐射建模加以厘清。

Method: 基于Illustris和IllustrisTNG宇宙学流体模拟，构建包含方向依赖尘埃衰减及Lyman系光子传播物理模型的Hα辐射模型；区分并计算三类Hα辐射源：恒星形成区（H II区）、弥散ISM中的碰撞激发与复合、以及AGN光致电离；使用单自由参数（SFR–Hα关系）在z∼1–1.5对观测Hα LF进行校准。

Result: 弥散气体贡献总Hα光度的约1%–10%；AGN光致电离仅轻微提升LF亮端，但在Illustris中主导L_Hα ≳ 3×10⁴² erg/s的星系；低反馈的IllustrisTNG能同时匹配z∼1时观测LF的亮端与暗端，而高反馈的Illustris则无法兼顾；该差异与kSZ堆积测量存在张力。

Conclusion: Hα光度函数对重子反馈强度高度敏感，可作为独立且有力的约束工具；结合kSZ等其他探针，有望协同限制反馈物理、ISM性质及发射线星系的辐射机制。

Abstract: Baryonic feedback remains one of the primary uncertainties in galaxy formation models because of its impact on the interstellar medium (ISM) and star formation. We investigate its effect on the H$α$ luminosity function (LF) using the Illustris and IllustrisTNG simulations, incorporating direction-dependent dust attenuation and a physically motivated treatment of the propagation of Lyman-series photons. We model three sources of H$α$ emission: star-forming H II regions, collisional excitation and recombination in the diffuse ISM, and AGN-induced photo-excitation of diffuse gas. The models are calibrated against observed H$α$ LFs at $z\sim 1-1.5$ using a single free parameter relating the star formation rate to the H II-region H$α$ luminosity. We find that emission from diffuse gas contributes $\sim 1\%-10\%$ of the total H$α$ luminosity in both simulation sets. AGN photo-excitation produces only a modest enhancement of the bright end of the LF, although it dominates the H$α$ emission of Illustris galaxies with $L_{\text{H}α}\gtrsim 3\times10^{42}\ \rm{erg \ s^{-1}}$. The low-feedback IllustrisTNG simulations provide good agreement with the observed LFs for all emission models considered, whereas the high-feedback Illustris simulation fails to simultaneously reproduce the faint and bright ends of the LF at $z\sim 1$. This tension between H$α$ LFs and stacked kSZ measurements highlights the combined power of these probes for constraining the interplay between baryonic feedback, gas properties, and the physics governing emission-line galaxies.

</details>


### [120] [Magnetohydrodynamics of charged interstellar dust. Multifluid models and study of the linear modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30711)
*Gabriel Verrier,Patrick Hennebelle,Ugo Lebreuilly,Valentin Vallucci-Goy*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑尘埃颗粒惯性的带电尘埃多流体模型，用于研究原恒星坍缩过程中尘埃、气体与磁场的耦合动力学；通过分析阿尔芬波和磁声波，明确了不同组分的耦合/退耦条件，并给出了依赖于尘埃分布的磁流体波速解析表达式；该模型在大尺度上与非理想MHD一致，同时拓展至尘埃惯性起关键作用的新物理区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星际尘埃颗粒在恒星与行星形成中起关键作用，其行为依赖于局地尘埃尺寸分布，需采用带电尘埃多流体方法；现有模型（如中性尘埃多流体或标准非理想磁流体）未能充分刻画尘埃惯性与电荷平衡对耦合动力学的影响。

Method: 构建了考虑尘埃惯性的带电尘埃多流体模型，结合化学网络模拟原恒星核坍缩中的电荷平衡；计算阿尔芬波与磁声波模态以分析气体、多尘埃流体与磁场之间的耦合机制；并与中性尘埃多流体及非理想磁流体模型进行对比分析。

Result: 发现高荷质比尘埃可主导原恒星包层中磁流体波的传播；推导出依赖于尘埃分布的波速解析表达式；磁压缩扰动可在天文单位尺度上引发局部尘气比变化；模型在大尺度上与非理想MHD一致，且成功拓展至尘埃惯性不可忽略的新参数区域。

Conclusion: 本文建立了线性区带电尘埃多流体理论框架，为数值模拟原恒星坍缩中的非线性效应（如湍流、角动量输运、磁致尘埃聚团）提供了可实施的闭合磁流体方程组。

Abstract: Interstellar grains play key roles in star and planet formation, including the coupling of the gas to the magnetic field during the protostellar collapse. These roles depend on the local grain size distribution, which requires a multifluid treatment of charged dust. We aim to understand the fundamental physics of the dynamics of a dust distribution in interaction with the gas and the magnetic field. In particular, the purpose is to characterize the (de)coupling conditions of these different components. We provide a multifluid model of charged dust which accounts for the inertia of the grains. A chemical network is used to simulate the charge equilibrium in collapsing protostellar cores. We compute the Alfven modes and the magnetosonic modes to understand the coupling regimes between the gas, the dust fluids, and the magnetic field. We also analyze and compare to the predictions of existing models, that are the neutral dust multifluid and the standard non-ideal magnetohydrodynamics. The charged multifluid model agrees with non-ideal magnetohydrodynamics on the larger scales of a collapsing dense core and the forming disk, while we successfully extend to new regimes where the inertia of dust grains matters. We found that high charge-to-mass dust grains carry the propagation of magnetohydrodynamical waves in protostellar envelopes. We provide analytical expressions of the speed of these waves depending on the dust distribution. The magnetocompressive perturbations lead to local dust-to-gas ratio variations at au scales. A theoretical understanding of the dynamics of a charged dust distribution is provided in the linear regime. The closed set of magnetohydrodynamics equations can be implemented in numerical codes to explore nonlinear effects during the protostellar collapse such as turbulence, angular momentum transport and magnetic dust clumping.

</details>


### [121] [Stellar streams around dwarf galaxies are observationally rare in the local Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30806)
*Joanna D. Sakowska,David Martíınez-Delgado,Sarah Pearson,Francisco J. Riquel-Castilla,Tjitske K. Starkenburg,Giuseppe Donatiello,Alis Deason,Denis Erkal,Ethan D. Taylor*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了首个系统性搜寻矮星系周围恒星流的巡天项目，利用DECam成像数据（DES和DECaLS）定义了卫星吸积特征分类指标，发现矮星系周围吸积特征（如星流、壳层、不对称晕）观测频率很低（5.1%），且未在DES中确认任何确凿的恒星流（仅在DECaLS中发现一个），提示星流在本地宇宙中确实稀少，可能反映不同质量星系间并合率的本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矮星系周围的恒星流在观测上仍缺乏统计显著的样本，因其尺寸小、亮度低、数量多，系统探测困难；亟需建立可靠方法并开展系统搜寻以理解其形成与演化。

Method: 提出一种基于DECam影像（来自DES和DECaLS巡天）的卫星吸积特征（星流、壳层、不对称恒星晕）分类指标，并在DES天区范围内系统应用该指标进行搜寻与统计分析。

Result: 在DES天区内发现吸积特征总体频率为5.1%，但未确认任何确凿恒星流；仅在DECaLS中识别出1个候选星流；壳层相对星流比例偏高，可能受观测偏差影响。

Conclusion: 本地宇宙中矮星系周围的恒星流极为稀少，这一观测结果可能真实反映低质量星系并合率较低，亟需理论工作进一步验证不同质量星系间的并合历史差异。

Abstract: The frequency and properties of stellar streams around dwarf galaxies remain observationally under-explored. Building a statistically significant sample is challenging because dwarf galaxies are smaller, fainter, and far more numerous than massive galaxies. We present the first results from a systematic survey for stellar streams around dwarf galaxies. We first introduce a classification metric for satellite accretion features (streams, shells, and asymmetric stellar haloes) using DECam imaging from the DES and DECaLS surveys. Applying this metric to the DES footprint, we find a low observed frequency of accretion features (5.1$\%$) and no bona fide stellar streams (with only one identified in DECaLS). We consider whether the apparent excess of shells relative to streams can be explained by observational biases. Our results indicate that stellar streams are observationally rare in the local Universe, motivating further theoretical work to determine whether this reflects genuine differences in merger rates across galaxy mass regimes.

</details>


### [122] [ALMA CO(2-1) Gas Dynamics in NGC 315: A Multi-Method Benchmark for Supermassive Black Hole Mass Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31015)
*Dieu D. Nguyen,Benjamin D. Boizelle,Hai N. Ngo,Elena Gallo,Tuan N. Le,Sabine Thater,Tien H. T. Ho,Tinh Q. T. Le,Que T. Le,Sam Norcross,Xueyi Li,Huy G. Tong,Nghi K. N. Le,Huy M. B. Tran*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用ALMA Cycle 7对NGC 315星系核周盘的CO(2-1)观测，以高角分辨率（0.230″×0.175″）解析了超大质量黑洞（SMBH）的影响球，并通过多种独立气体动力学建模方法（贝叶斯MCMC、嵌套采样与频率学方法）一致地测得黑洞质量为2.02×10⁹ M⊙，系统误差与统计误差相当；结果支持经验标度关系，但略低于恒星动力学测量值，凸显气体与恒星方法间的系统差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定超大质量黑洞质量并评估不同动力学方法（尤其是气体 vs 恒星）带来的系统不确定性，确立NGC 315作为分子气体动力学测BH质量的基准源。

Method: 基于ALMA Cycle 7高分辨率CO(2-1)数据，对核周盘分子气体进行全数据立方体前向建模；采用多种独立建模技术（MCMC、嵌套采样、频率学方法），系统考察恒星质量分布、气体面亮度参数化及盘几何结构等建模假设带来的系统误差。

Result: 所有建模方法一致给出黑洞质量M_BH = 2.02^{+0.04}_{-0.05}(stat)^{+0.05}_{-0.04}(sys) × 10⁹ M⊙；系统误差与统计误差量级相当；该质量与M_BH–σ⋆和M_BH–L_bulge标度关系一致，但比独立恒星动力学测量低32%。

Conclusion: 分子气体动力学方法在高分辨率下可稳健、一致地测定黑洞质量，其主要不确定性源于建模系统误差而非拟合统计误差；NGC 315成为量化气体动力学系统误差及未来交叉验证气体与恒星方法的关键基准。

Abstract: We present ALMA Cycle~7 \cotwo\ observations of the circumnuclear disk in NGC~315 at an angular resolution of $0\farcs230\times0\farcs175$, improving on past measurements and resolving the sphere of influence (SOI) of the supermassive black hole (SMBH), whose mass has previously been estimated of $M_{\rm BH}= \left(2.08^{+0.33}_{-0.15}\right) \times 10^9$~M$_\odot$ The high spatial resolution and sensitivity enable robust full-cube forward modeling of the molecular gas kinematics and a direct comparison of multiple independent gas-based dynamical modeling techniques. We apply standard Bayesian codes using both MCMC and nested sampling approaches, as well as a frequentist code to the same dataset, exploring systematic uncertainties associated with the stellar mass distribution, gas surface-brightness parameterization, and disk geometry. All methods yield consistent black hole masses, indicating that the inferred $M_{\rm BH}$ is not strongly method-dependent. Combining the ensemble of independent molecular-gas-based models, we derive an ensemble median black hole mass of $M_{\rm BH}/10^9\,\mathrm{M_\odot} = 2.02^{+0.04}_{-0.05}$(stat)$^{+0.05}_{-0.04}$(sys), where the comparable contributions to the full error budget arise from modeling systematics rather than formal fitting uncertainties. Our $M_{\rm BH}$ is consistent with the empirical $M_{\rm BH}$--$σ_\star$ and $M_{\rm BH}$--$L_{\rm bulge}$ scaling relations, and lies 32\% below an independent stellar-dynamical measurement, a discrepancy we discuss in the context of systematic differences between gas- and stellar-based methods. NGC~315 serves as a benchmark for quantifying molecular gas-dynamical $M_{\rm BH}$ systematic uncertainties and for future cross-comparisons of gaseous and stellar dynamical approaches.

</details>


### [123] [The impact of hot mode accretion and angular momentum conservation on metallicity gradients of galactic discs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31144)
*Jennifer K. S. Friske,Filippo Fraternali,Gabriele Pezzulli*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种半解析化学演化模型，结合宇宙学演化、自洽时变势阱和等温旋转热星系周介质（CGM），研究径向流对星系气体金属丰度梯度的影响，并通过贝叶斯拟合银河系观测数据，反推CGM温度与旋转特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测到的星系金属丰度梯度蕴含吸积气体运动学信息，但以往模型将角动量失配简单参数化，未从CGM自身性质出发；本文旨在建立更物理自洽的模型以揭示热CGM吸积对金属梯度的驱动机制。

Method: 构建包含宇宙学演化、时变引力势和等温旋转CGM的半解析化学演化模型，纳入由势阱加深和垂直吸积共同引发的径向流，并采用贝叶斯框架联合拟合星系结构参数与金属丰度梯度。

Result: 对银河系的拟合表明CGM温度约为维里温度的0.82倍（存在约2σ张力），当前CGM旋转速度约100–160 km/s且随时间下降，预测的金属梯度演化在过去8 Gyr内与观测一致。

Conclusion: 该模型为约束星系盘的热模吸积提供了新方法，不仅适用于银河系，也可推广至外部星系，将金属梯度作为探测CGM动力学的关键探针。

Abstract: The hot circumgalactic medium (CGM) of a galaxy inevitably rotates more slowly than the cold gas in the disc it surrounds, due to a higher pressure support against gravity. If it accretes vertically onto the disc, angular momentum conservation leads to radial flows, advection of metals inwards and the steepening of a metallicity gradient. The observed gradient hence carries information on the kinematics of the accreting gas. Previous models built on this premise parametrised the angular momentum mismatch as a simple function of radius, constant in time, rather than derived from the properties of the CGM itself. Here, we present a semi-analytic chemical evolution model of a galaxy with a cosmologically motivated evolution, a self-consistent and time-evolving potential and an isothermal rotating CGM. The model includes inside-out formation and the radial flows induced both by the deepening of the potential well (acting on gas and stars) and by accretion, following their combined effect on the gas-phase metallicity gradient over cosmic time. We use a Bayesian framework to fit simultaneously the structural properties and metallicity gradient of a galaxy, inferring both the required inside-out growth and the CGM temperature needed to drive the required radial flows and abundance gradient. Fitting our model to the Milky Way, we find a mild preference for a subvirial corona ($T_\mathrm{CGM}/T_{200}=0.82^{+0.44}_{-0.38}$), in mild tension with observations, and we discuss possible resolutions. The predicted CGM rotation velocity lies between ~100-160 km/s at the present time and is substantially lower at earlier epochs. We further recover an evolution of the gradient over the past ~8Gyr consistent with observations. Our approach is not restricted to the Milky Way and can be directly applied to external galaxies, offering a new handle to constrain hot mode accretion onto galactic discs.

</details>

### [124] [Machine Learning Unveils Finite-volume Energy Shifts in Three-body System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28700)
*Wei-Jie Zhang,Zhenyu Zhang,Jifeng Hu,Bing-Nan Lu,Jin-Yi Pang,Qian Wang*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合符号回归（PySR）与物理约束的数据驱动方法，用于推导适用于短程和长程相互作用的通用三体有限体积外推（FVE）公式，尤其在约1 fm的中间力程范围内表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限体积外推方法在处理力程与格点尺寸L相当的长程相互作用时存在困难，亟需新方法来统一描述短程与长程情形下的三体FVE行为。

Method: 基于先前两体数据驱动方案，采用符号回归（PySR）从数据中挖掘普适的三体FVE公式，并融合物理约束构建统一解析表达式。

Result: 成功复现短程极限情况（κ₃≫κ₂和κ₃∼κ₂），获得纯长程势的专用解析式，并导出覆盖中间力程（~1 fm）的统一FVE公式，精度优异。

Conclusion: 机器学习与物理先验结合可发现传统理论工具难以得到的新解析规律，为强子物理中的数据驱动研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: Finite-volume extrapolation (FVE) is essential for extracting physical observables in the lattice calculation. While rigorous FVE formulations are well established for short-range potentials in both two- and three-body systems, long-range interactions with force ranges comparable to the lattice size $L$ remain challenging. Extending a previous data-driven scheme for two-body systems, we apply symbolic regression (PySR) to uncover universal three-body FVE formulae. For short-range potentials, we reproduce the two limiting cases, i.e. $κ_3\ggκ_2$ and $κ_3\simκ_2$. For pure long-range potentials, we obtain a dedicated analytic expression, and after incorporating short-range contributions, we uncover a unified formula consistent with the original PySR solution, which performs excellently in the intermediate force range around 1 fm. This work demonstrates that combining machine learning with physical constraints can yield novel analytical results inaccessible to conventional theoretical tools, advancing data-driven methodologies in hadron physics.

</details>


### [125] [Quantum Simulations of Two-Dimensional Non-Abelian Adjoint String Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28752)
*Anthony N. Ciavarella,Roland de Putter,Ed Younis,Ermal Rrapaj*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部Krylov的SU(2)格点规范理论截断方法，在三角晶格上构建了与动力学胶子相连的伴随弦，并利用IBM Boston量子处理器（156量子比特）在8×8和16×8格点上实现了伴随弦断裂的量子模拟，结果与张量网络模拟定量一致，并揭示了非阿贝尔规范群的独特特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用量子计算机直接探测强耦合量子场论动力学的潜力，但需先发展适用于量子硬件的可实现理论截断方案。

Method: 采用局部Krylov方法对纯SU(2)三角晶格规范理论进行截断；构造伴随弦及关联动力学胶子；设计局域算符识别弦振荡与断裂；在IBM Boston超导量子处理器上实现大规模量子线路模拟。

Result: 在156量子比特设备上成功模拟伴随弦断裂；7634个CZ门、双量子比特门深度218的线路与张量网络结果定量吻合；观测到弦振荡与胶球产生速率，呈现SU(2)非阿贝尔规范群的明确特征。

Conclusion: 局部Krylov截断是连接格点规范理论与当前含噪声中等规模量子硬件的有效桥梁，该工作为强耦合场论的量子模拟提供了可行路径与关键验证。

Abstract: Quantum computers offer the potential to directly probe the dynamics of strongly coupled quantum field theories. As a step towards reaching this potential, local Krylov-based truncations of a pure SU(2) lattice gauge theory on a triangular lattice are constructed. Adjoint strings connected to dynamical gluons are constructed in this truncated theory, and the resonances dominating the long-term dynamics at a large value of the gauge coupling are determined. Local operators are constructed to identify string oscillations and breakings. This is used to perform a quantum simulation of adjoint string breaking on an $8\times8$ and $16\times8$-site lattice with ibm_boston using all 156 qubits. Quantitative agreement with tensor network simulations is obtained for circuits with 7,634 CZ gates with a two-qubit gate depth of 218. In this simulation, the rates of oscillations and glueball production are identified with a distinctly non-Abelian signature of the underlying gauge group.

</details>


### [126] [Detecting Multiple Phase Transitions in Lattice Systems with Intrinsic Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29090)
*Jie Mei,Tetsuo Hatsuda,Mei Huang,Lingxiao Wang*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出使用蒙特卡洛构型集合的内禀维度（通过双近邻法估计）作为几何诊断工具，以区分多相变格点系统中不同尺度的相变并识别各相变主导的自由度。在二维q态钟模型和四维U(1)希格斯模型中验证了该方法的有效性，并强调了去除规范冗余对几何分析的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有多个邻近相变的格点系统中两个相关挑战：分辨不同的相变尺度，以及识别与每个相变主要关联的自由度。

Method: 采用双近邻法估计蒙特卡洛构型集合的内禀维度，并在二维q态钟模型和四维U(1)希格斯模型中引入基于规范不变变量（如圈变量和希格斯场）的通道分解内禀维度；同时对比了规范协变场上的直接计算结果。

Result: 在q=9和q=5的二维钟模型中，内禀维度成功区分了有序、准临界和无序相；尤其在q=5时，中间相表现为宽而低维的‘谷’，即使能量和磁化强度无法单独分辨两个BKT相变；在四维U(1)希格斯模型中，通道分解内禀维度能分别追踪对应自由度的相变行为，而组合通道保留两者特征；规范协变场上的内禀维度则被规范轨道方向主导。

Conclusion: 通道分解的内禀维度是一种有效的几何探针，适用于含多重相变的格点系统，有望用于QCD中解禁闭与手征交叉尺度的分离研究。

Abstract: Lattice systems with multiple nearby transitions pose two related challenges: resolving distinct transition scales and identifying the degrees of freedom primarily associated with each transition. We show that the intrinsic dimension of Monte Carlo configuration ensembles, estimated by the two-nearest-neighbors method, provides a geometric diagnostic for both problems. In the two-dimensional $q$-state clock model, the intrinsic dimension distinguishes the ordered, quasi-critical, and disordered regimes for both well-separated ($q=9$) and closely spaced ($q=5$) Berezinskii--Kosterlitz--Thouless transitions. In the $q=5$ case, the intermediate phase appears as a broad low-dimensional valley even when energy and magnetization do not separately resolve the two transitions. In the four-dimensional $U(1)$ Higgs model, we introduce channel-decomposed intrinsic dimensions based on gauge-invariant plaquette and Higgs variables. The dominant response of each channel tracks transitions associated with the corresponding degrees of freedom, while the combined channel retains features of both. We further show that intrinsic dimensions evaluated directly on gauge-variant fields are dominated by gauge-orbit directions, demonstrating the importance of removing gauge redundancy before interpreting configuration-space geometry. These results establish channel-decomposed intrinsic dimension as a geometric probe of lattice systems with multiple transitions and motivate its application to disentangling deconfinement and chiral crossover scales in full QCD.

</details>


### [127] [Symmetric Mass Generation for Domain-Wall Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29963)
*Sho Araki,Hidenori Fukaya,Tetsuya Onogi,Satoshi Yamaguchi*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次数值研究了域墙费米子的对称质量生成（SMG），通过二维欧几里得格点模拟Fidkowski-Kitaev马约拉纳链，发现四费米子相互作用可选择性地在镜像壁上打开能隙，而保持物理壁无隙；其特征是狄拉克算符复本征谱沿虚轴开隙，而非实轴平移；重加权小复相位后传统HMC算法仍适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现格点域墙费米子中镜像费米子的非微扰、相互作用诱导解耦，需探索对称质量生成（SMG）机制的可行性与特征。

Method: 在二维欧几里得格点上模拟具双边界（物理壁与镜像壁）的Fidkowski-Kitaev马约拉纳链，计算路径积分；分析格点狄拉克算符本征谱；采用带Pfaffian复相位重加权的混合蒙特卡洛（HMC）算法。

Result: 四费米子相互作用选择性地在镜像壁上打开边缘费米子能隙，物理壁保持无隙；狄拉克算符能隙沿复本征谱虚轴开启，区别于常规实质量项；Pfaffian复相位极小，重加权HMC算法仍高效可行。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个实用的非微扰框架，证实SMG可用于域墙费米子中镜像费米子的有效解耦，为消除格点规范理论中手征异常问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: We report the first numerical examination of symmetric mass generation (SMG) for domain-wall fermions. Our simulation on a two-dimensional Euclidean lattice computes the path-integral of the Fidkowski-Kitaev Majorana chain with two boundaries, corresponding to the two (physical and mirror) walls. We demonstrate by evaluating the lattice Dirac operator eigenvalue spectrum that the local four-Fermi interaction selectively gaps the edge fermions on the mirror wall while leaving the physical wall intact. We further identify a characteristic signature of SMG in the path-integral: the gap of the Dirac operator opens along the imaginary axis of the complex spectrum, in contrast to the standard mass shift in the real direction. The conventional hybrid Monte Carlo algorithm remains practical with reweighting the complex phase of the Pfaffian, which is found to be remarkably small throughout the simulation. These results provide a practical nonperturbative framework for interaction-induced mirror-fermion decoupling of lattice domain-wall fermions.

</details>


### [128] [Bias-Corrected Machine-Learning Estimation of Chiral Condensate Cumulants: A Retrospective Lattice QCD Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30416)
*Benjamin J. Choi,Hiroshi Ohno,Akio Tomiya*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过回顾性案例研究，评估了在格点QCD中使用偏差校正的机器学习方法估计Dirac算子逆的迹（Tr M^{-n}）的效果，并分析其在手征凝聚子矩和多组系重加权中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高格点QCD中高成本Dirac算子求逆计算的效率，同时保持统计精度，特别是在非线性衍生量（如累积量和重加权）中控制偏差。

Method: 采用两种监督学习方法：一种以Tr M^{-1}为输入特征，另一种以规范观测量（如plaquette、rectangle）为输入；并引入偏差校正策略；在固定数据集上系统改变标注/训练集比例进行评估。

Result: 偏差校正后的ML估计与全数据基准高度一致；未校正估计在累积量和重加权步骤后偏差显著放大；以Tr M^{-1}为特征的方法在求逆次数上可节省至原计算的约25.75%（名义估算）。

Conclusion: 偏差校正对保障ML在格点QCD非线性物理量估算中的可靠性至关重要；基于Tr M^{-1}的ML方案具备显著降低Dirac求逆开销的潜力，但需进一步端到端验证。

Abstract: We present a retrospective case study of bias-corrected machine learning (ML) estimates of traces of the inverse Dirac operator, $\text{Tr}\,M^{-n}$ ($n=1,2,3,4$), using a fixed lattice QCD dataset and examining how the results depend on the relative proportions of the labeled and training sets. Two supervised learning approaches are examined: one using $\text{Tr}\,M^{-1}$ as the input feature, and the other employing gauge observables such as the plaquette and rectangle. Beyond the direct estimation of $\text{Tr}\,M^{-n}$, we further investigate two derived applications of the ML estimations: the evaluation of the cumulants of the chiral condensate within a single ensemble and that obtained through multi-ensemble reweighting across ensembles with different quark masses. Within this fixed dataset, the bias-corrected estimates show close agreement with the full-data reference under the adopted evaluation criteria, while the uncorrected estimates can exhibit amplified deviations after the nonlinear cumulant and reweighting steps. For the approach using $\text{Tr}\,M^{-1}$ as the input feature, nominal solve-count accounting suggests that the Dirac-inversion cost could be reduced to approximately $25.75\%$ of that of the conventional calculation in the present setup. This value is a cost projection rather than an end-to-end benchmark: it assumes comparable costs for successive inversions and excludes model-training and analysis overhead.

</details>


### [129] [$D \to (K π)_{\mathbf{27}}$ at the SU(3)-flavour-symmetric point I: Methodology and strong phase determination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30737)
*Matthew Black,Felix Erben,Maxwell T. Hansen,Fabian Joswig,Nelson Pitanga Lachini,Rajnandini Mukherjee,Srijit Paul,Antonin Portelli*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文是SU(3)味对称格点QCD计算的第一部分，旨在研究D介子衰变到Kπ末态（27维表示）的强相互作用振幅，通过多组格点参数提取散射相移，并外推至D介子质量处得到强相位δ₂₇≈−38.4°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定D→Kπ衰变中27维表示的强相位，为理解CP破坏和检验标准模型提供关键输入；利用SU(3)味对称性简化计算并控制系统误差。

Method: 采用OpenLat生成的等质量π/K（≈410 MeV）Wilson–clover格点系综，结合蒸馏方法构造动量投影单强子关联函数矩阵；解广义本征值问题获得有限体积能谱，再用Lüscher方法提取散射相移；在三个格距下分别实施两种连续极限外推策略（相移外推 vs 能谱外推）。

Result: 获得能量依赖的27维散射相移δ₂₇(E_cm)，在SU(3)对称D介子质量（≈1900 MeV）处得出强相位δ₂₇ = −38.4(2.4)°；验证了两种连续极限外推方法结果一致；并阐述了后续计算三定点函数⟨(Kπ)₂₇|H_W|D⟩以获取完整衰变振幅的方法框架。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在格点QCD中系统计算了D→Kπ（27维）的强相互作用相位，为后续结合弱哈密顿量完成完整衰变振幅计算奠定基础；所用方法具有可推广性，适用于其他味改变过程的强相位研究。

Abstract: We present part one of an SU(3)-flavour-symmetric lattice QCD calculation of the amplitude for a $D$-meson decaying to a $Kπ$ final state in the 27-dimensional irreducible representation of the flavour symmetry group, denoted $(Kπ)_{\mathbf{27}}$. The Wilson--clover gauge ensembles used in this work, generated by the OpenLat collaboration, are tuned such that $M_π= M_K \approx 410\,\mathrm{MeV}$. Using the distillation framework, we construct a matrix of Euclidean correlation functions from pairs of single-hadron operators projected to definite spatial momentum. Solving a generalised eigenvalue problem yields the finite-volume energy spectrum that is used to determine the scattering phase shift from threshold up to $4 M_π\approx 1640 \,\mathrm{MeV}$, which sits below but plausibly within reach of $M_D^{\rm SU(3)} \simeq 1900\,\mathrm{MeV}$. The calculation is performed across three lattice spacings, and we apply two strategies in which the continuum limit is taken at different stages of the computation: (i) on the extracted scattering parameters and (ii) on the finite-volume energies at fixed physical volume before extracting the scattering parameters. We find consistent results across these methods for the scattering phase shift as a function of the centre-of-mass energy, $δ_{\mathbf{27}}(E_{\sf cm})$. Taking a scattering-length-only parametrisation, we infer a value for the strong phase of the weak decay, $δ_{\mathbf{27}}(M_D^{\rm SU(3)})=-38.4(2.4)^\circ$. We further describe the methodology for using the same operator basis to compute three-point correlation functions to extract $\langle (Kπ)_{\mathbf{27}}| H_W| D\rangle$, for the tree-level effective weak Hamiltonian $H_W$, and for relating such finite-volume matrix elements to the full decay amplitude. The complete analysis leading to the latter will be presented in a forthcoming manuscript.

</details>


### [130] [First-principles determination of anomaly-induced pion decay beyond the chiral limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31024)
*Tian Lin,Xu Feng,Lu-Chang Jin,Chuan Liu,Qi-Yuan Luo*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过格点QCD计算了中性π介子双光子衰变中由夸克质量引起的修正，利用反常PCAC关系避免了传统方法中的四点函数和手征对数抵消问题，实现了约1%的统计精度，并首次从第一性原理确认了π⁰-η-η'混合增强效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性π介子双光子衰变在手征极限下由Adler-Bell-Jackiw反常严格确定，但非零夸克质量会引入百分之几的修正，需精确计算以检验超出手征极限的QCD理论。

Method: 利用反常PCAC关系，在q²=0处计算偏离精确反常条件的修正；采用近物理参数的域墙费米子格点QCD组态，避免四点函数及手征对数相消问题。

Result: 在两个近物理域墙费米子组态上分别达到约1%统计精度，连续极限外推后得到Γ(π⁰→γγ)=8.09(22) eV；轻夸克质量修正使衰变振幅增加2.3(1.4)%，主要源于同位旋破缺，首次从第一性原理证实π⁰-η-η'混合增强。

Conclusion: 该方法显著压制了统计与系统误差，为高精度检验QCD提供了新途径，并首次从第一性原理定量支持了π⁰-η-η'混合增强机制。

Abstract: The two-photon decay of the neutral pion is fixed in the chiral limit by the Adler-Bell-Jackiw anomaly, while nonzero quark masses induce few-percent corrections that must be determined for precision tests of QCD beyond the chiral limit. Using the anomalous PCAC relation, we compute them in lattice QCD as deviations from the exact anomaly condition at $q^2=0$, thereby avoiding both four-point functions and the cancellation of chiral logarithms that limits conventional approaches. Because the correction is proportional to the light-quark masses, both statistical and systematic uncertainties are correspondingly suppressed, making a precision calculation feasible. On two nearly-physical domain-wall ensembles we achieve $\sim1\%$ statistical precision each for the decay width, obtaining $Γ(π^0\toγγ)=8.09(22) eV$ after continuum extrapolation. The $2.3(1.4)\%$ mass correction to the decay amplitude, positive and isospin-breaking dominated, provides the first ab initio confirmation of the $π^0$-$η$-$η'$ mixing enhancement.

</details>
